Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a fundamental_group algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 282. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 511/3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_282(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 282. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^511(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any fundamental_group module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 272. The projective resolution has length at most 887 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the fundamental_group space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 272 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 887. QED.
